> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

The frontend (1) skips all standalone tool_result turns (line 41-43), (2) groups tool_call + paired tool_result into accordion widgets, and (3) already skips the duplicate assistant turn via dedup at lines 58-70. So currently, message_user text ONLY appears inside the tool accordion \u2014 the duplicate assistant turn is already invisible. Removing it from the backend changes nothing visible. This is safe.\n\n**Deno import paths:** All imports use relative paths with .ts extensions (Deno convention). New modules must follow this: `import { foo } from \"./types.ts\"`, `import { bar } from \"./llm/client.ts\"`, etc. The shared imports use `../_shared/` paths.\n\n**The timelineState mutation pattern:** In the current executeTimelineTool (index.ts:426-497), the function MUTATES the timelineState object in place (state.config = ..., state.configVersion = ...). This is intentional \u2014 the loop uses a single timelineState reference across iterations. Preserve this mutation pattern in the registry.\n\n**File layout decision (settled):** db.ts is a separate module (user chose this). config.ts holds all tunables (user chose this). These differ from the plan which discussed alternatives.\n\n**What NOT to change:** prompts.ts stays as-is. The tool schema definitions (tools.ts \u2192 tool-schemas.ts) stay as-is except the rename. The withEdgeRequest handler pattern in index.ts stays the same. The HTTP response format stays the same."
}

- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

};\n+}\n+\n+export function setClipProperty(\n+  config: TimelineConfig,\n+  _registry: AssetRegistry,\n+  args: { clipId?: string; property?: string; value?: unknown },\n+): TimelineToolResult {\n+  if (\n+    typeof args.clipId !== \"string\"\n+    || typeof args.property !== \"string\"\n+    || typeof args.value !== \"number\"\n+  ) {\n+    return { result: \"set_clip_property requires clipId, property, and numeric value.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  if (!MUTABLE_CLIP_PROPERTIES.includes(args.property as ClipProperty)) {\n+    return {\n+      result: `Property ${args.property} is not allowed. Use one of ${MUTABLE_CLIP_PROPERTIES.join(\", \")}.`,\n+    };\n+  }\n+\n+  const nextConfig = cloneConfig(config);\n+  const clipIndex = getClipIndex(nextConfig, args.clipId);\n+  if (clipIndex < 0) {\n+    return { result: `Clip ${args.clipId} was not found.` };\n+  }\n+\n+  const clip = nextConfig.clips[clipIndex] as TimelineClip & Record<ClipProperty, number | undefined>;\n+  const previousValue = clip[args.property as ClipProperty];\n+  clip[args.property as ClipProperty] = args.value;\n+\n+  return {\n+    config: nextConfig,\n+    result: `Set ${args.property} on clip ${clip.id} from ${previousValue ?? \"unset\"} to ${args.value}.`,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function deleteClip(\n+  config: TimelineConfig,\n+  _registry: AssetRegistry,\n+  args: { clipId?: string },\n+): TimelineToolResult {\n+  if (typeof args.clipId !== \"string\") {\n+    return { result: \"delete_clip requires clipId.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  const clip = config.clips.find((item) => item.id === args.clipId);\n+  if (!clip) {\n+    return { result: `Clip ${args.clipId} was not found.` };\n+  }\n+\n+  const nextConfig = cloneConfig(config);\n+  nextConfig.clips = nextConfig.clips.filter((item) => item.id !== args.clipId);\n+\n+  return {\n+    config: nextConfig,\n+    result: `Deleted clip ${clip.id} on track ${clip.track}.`,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function addTextClip(\n+  config: TimelineConfig,\n+  _registry: AssetRegistry,\n+  args: { track?: string; at?: number; duration?: number; text?: string },\n+): TimelineToolResult {\n+  if (\n+    typeof args.track !== \"string\"\n+    || typeof args.at !== \"number\"\n+    || typeof args.duration !== \"number\"\n+    || typeof args.text !== \"string\"\n+  ) {\n+    return { result: \"add_text_clip requires track, at, duration, and text.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  if (args.duration <= 0) {\n+    return { result: \"add_text_clip duration must be greater than 0.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  const tracks = getTrackDefinitions(config);\n+  if (tracks.length > 0 && !tracks.some((track) => track.id === args.track)) {\n+    return { result: `Track ${args.track} does not exist.` };\n+  }\n+\n+  const nextConfig = cloneConfig(config);\n+  const clipId = crypto.randomUUID();\n+  nextConfig.clips.push({\n+    id: clipId,\n+    at: roundSeconds(args.at),\n+    track: args.track,\n+    clipType: \"text\",\n+    hold: roundSeconds(args.duration),\n+    text: {\n+      content: args.text,\n+    },\n+  });\n+\n+  return {\n+    config: nextConfig,\n+    result: `Added text clip ${clipId} on track ${args.track} at ${roundSeconds(args.at)}s for ${roundSeconds(args.duration)}s.`,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function findIssues(config: TimelineConfig, registry: AssetRegistry): TimelineToolResult {\n+  const issues: string[] = [];\n+  const clipsByTrack = new Map<string, TimelineClip[]>();\n+\n+  for (const clip of config.clips) {\n+    const currentTrackClips = clipsByTrack.get(clip.track) ?? [];\n+    currentTrackClips.push(clip);\n+    clipsByTrack.set(clip.track, currentTrackClips);\n+\n+    const assetDuration = getAssetDuration(registry, clip.asset);\n+    const clipSourceEnd = typeof clip.to === \"number\"\n+      ? clip.to\n+      : typeof clip.hold === \"number\"\n+        ? clip.hold\n+        : assetDuration ?? null;\n+\n+    if (assetDuration !== null && typeof clipSourceEnd === \"number\" && clipSourceEnd > assetDuration + 0.0001) {\n+      issues.push(\n+        `Clip ${clip.id} exceeds asset duration (${roundSeconds(clipSourceEnd)}s > ${roundSeconds(assetDuration)}s).`,\n+      );\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  for (const [trackId, trackClips] of clipsByTrack.entries()) {\n+    const sortedClips = [...trackClips].sort((left, right) => left.at - right.at);\n+\n+    for (let index = 1; index < sortedClips.length; index += 1) {\n+      const previousClip = sortedClips[index - 1];\n+      const currentClip = sortedClips[index];\n+      const previousEnd = previousClip.at + getClipTimelineDuration(previousClip, registry);\n+\n+      if (currentClip.at - previousEnd > 0.01) {\n+        issues.push(\n+          `Gap on track ${trackId} between ${previousClip.id} and ${currentClip.id}: ${roundSeconds(currentClip.at - previousEnd)}s.`,\n+        );\n+      }\n+\n+      if (previousEnd - currentClip.at > 0.01) {\n+        issues.push(\n+          `Overlap on track ${trackId} between ${previousClip.id} and ${currentClip.id}: ${roundSeconds(previousEnd - currentClip.at)}s.`,\n+        );\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  // Mid-sentence cut detection depends on transcript data that the current timeline provider does not expose.\n+  return {\n+    result: issues.length > 0 ? issues.join(\"\\n\") : \"No timeline issues found.\",\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export const timelineTools = {\n+  add_text_clip: addTextClip,\n+  delete_clip: deleteClip,\n+  find_issues: findIssues,\n+  move_clip: moveClip,\n+  set_clip_property: setClipProperty,\n+  trim_clip: trimClip,\n+  view_timeline: viewTimeline,\n+};\n+\n+export const handlers: Record<string, ToolHandler> = {\n+  add_text_clip: (args, ctx) => addTextClip(ctx.config, ctx.registry, args),\n+  delete_clip: (args, ctx) => deleteClip(ctx.config, ctx.registry, args),\n+  find_issues: (_args, ctx) => findIssues(ctx.config, ctx.registry),\n+  move_clip: (args, ctx) => moveClip(ctx.config, ctx.registry, args),\n+  set_clip_property: (args, ctx) => setClipProperty(ctx.config, ctx.registry, args),\n+  trim_clip: (args, ctx) => trimClip(ctx.config, ctx.registry, args),\n+  view_timeline: (_args, ctx) => viewTimeline(ctx.config, ctx.registry),\n+};\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/session.ts\n+import type { ToolHandler, ToolResult } from \"../types.ts\";\n+\n+export function messageUser(text: string): ToolResult {\n+  return {\n+    result: text,\n+    stopLoop: true,\n+    nextStatus: \"waiting_user\",\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function done(summary: string): ToolResult {\n+  return {\n+    result: summary,\n+    stopLoop: true,\n+    nextStatus: \"done\",\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export const handlers: Record<string, ToolHandler> = {\n+  done: (args) =>\n+    typeof args.summary === \"string\"\n+      ? done(args.summary)\n+      : { result: \"done requires a summary string.\" },\n+  message_user: (args) =>\n+    typeof args.text === \"string\"\n+      ? messageUser(args.text)\n+      : { result: \"message_user requires a text string.\" },\n+};\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts\n+import type { ToolHandler, ToolResult } from \"../types.ts\";\n+\n+export interface CreateGenerationTaskArgs {\n+  project_id?: string;\n+  prompt?: string;\n+  count?: number;\n+  task_type?: string;\n+  idempotency_key?: string;\n+  params?: Record<string, unknown>;\n+}\n+\n+function requireEnv(name: string): string {\n+  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\n+  if (!value) {\n+    throw new Error(`[ai-timeline-agent] Missing ${name}`);\n+  }\n+  return value;\n+}\n+\n+async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {\n+  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);\n+  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest(\"SHA-256\", encoded);\n+  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))\n+    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n+    .join(\"\");\n+}\n+\n+async function getIdempotencyKey(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs): Promise<string> {\n+  if (typeof args.idempotency_key === \"string\" && args.idempotency_key.trim()) {\n+    return args.idempotency_key.trim();\n+  }\n+\n+  const payload = JSON.stringify({\n+    project_id: args.project_id,\n+    task_type: args.task_type ?? \"image_generation\",\n+    params: args.params ?? { prompt: args.prompt, count: args.count ?? 1 },\n+  });\n+  const digest = await sha256Hex(payload);\n+  return `timeline-agent:${digest.slice(0, 40)}`;\n+}\n+\n+export async function createGenerationTask(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs): Promise<ToolResult> {\n+  if (typeof args.project_id !== \"string\" || !args.project_id.trim()) {\n+    return { result: \"create_generation_task requires project_id.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  const taskType = args.task_type ?? \"image_generation\";\n+  if (taskType !== \"image_generation\") {\n+    return { result: \"Only image_generation is supported by create_generation_task right now.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  const params = args.params\n+    ?? (typeof args.prompt === \"string\" && args.prompt.trim()\n+      ? {\n+          prompt: args.prompt.trim(),\n+          count: typeof args.count === \"number\" ? args.count : 1,\n+        }\n+      : null);\n+\n+  if (!params) {\n+    return { result: \"create_generation_task requires either params or prompt.\" };\n+  }\n+\n+  const supabaseUrl = requireEnv(\"SUPABASE_URL\");\n+  const serviceRoleKey = requireEnv(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\");\n+  const idempotencyKey = await getIdempotencyKey(args);\n+\n+  const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/create-task`, {\n+    method: \"POST\",\n+    headers: {\n+      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n+      Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceRoleKey}`,\n+      apikey: serviceRoleKey,\n+    },\n+    body: JSON.stringify({\n+      task_type: taskType,\n+      params,\n+      project_id: args.project_id.trim(),\n+      dependant_on: null,\n+      idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,\n+    }),\n+  });\n+\n+  if (!response.ok) {\n+    const errorText = await response.text().catch(() => \"\");\n+    const message = errorText || \"Failed to create task\";\n+    return { result: `Failed to create task: ${message}` };\n+  }\n+\n+  const data = await response.json() as { task_id?: string; deduplicated?: boolean };\n+\n+  return {\n+    result: data.task_id\n+      ? `Queued ${taskType} task ${data.task_id}${data.deduplicated ? \" (deduplicated).\" : \".\"}`\n+      : `Queued ${taskType} task.`,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export const handlers: Record<string, ToolHandler> = {\n+  create_generation_task: (args) => createGenerationTask(args),\n+};\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts\n+import type { TimelineConfig } from \"../../../src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts\";\n+import { isRecord, isSessionStatus, normalizeTimelineRow } from \"./llm/messages.ts\";\n+import type {\n+  AgentSessionStatus,\n+  AgentTurn,\n+  SupabaseAdmin,\n+  TimelineState,\n+} from \"./types.ts\";\n+\n+export async function loadTimelineState(\n+  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,\n+  timelineId: string,\n+): Promise<TimelineState> {\n+  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n+    .from(\"timelines\")\n+    .select(\"config, config_version, asset_registry, project_id\")\n+    .eq(\"id\", timelineId)\n+    .maybeSingle();\n+\n+  if (error) {\n+    throw new Error(`Failed to load timeline: ${error.message}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  if (!data || !isRecord(data)) {\n+    throw new Error(\"Timeline not found\");\n+  }\n+\n+  const normalized = normalizeTimelineRow(data);\n+  return {\n+    config: normalized.config,\n+    configVersion: normalized.config_version,\n+    registry: normalized.asset_registry,\n+    projectId: normalized.project_id,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export async function saveTimelineConfigVersioned(\n+  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,\n+  timelineId: string,\n+  expectedVersion: number,\n+  config: TimelineConfig,\n+): Promise<number | null> {\n+  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n+    .rpc(\"update_timeline_config_versioned\", {\n+      p_timeline_id: timelineId,\n+      p_expected_version: expectedVersion,\n+      p_config: config,\n+    })\n+    .maybeSingle();\n+\n+  if (error) {\n+    throw new Error(`Failed to save timeline config: ${error.message}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  const nextVersion = (data as { config_version?: unknown } | null)?.config_version;\n+  return typeof nextVersion === \"number\" ? nextVersion : null;\n+}\n+\n+export async function loadSessionStatus(\n+  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,\n+  sessionId: string,\n+): Promise<AgentSessionStatus | null> {\n+  const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin\n+    .from(\"timeline_agent_sessions\")\n+    .select(\"status\")\n+    .eq(\"id\", sessionId)\n+    .maybeSingle();\n+\n+  if (error || !data || !isRecord(data)) {\n+    return null;\n+  }\n+\n+  return isSessionStatus(data.status) ? data.status : null;\n+}\n+\n+export async function persistSessionState(\n+  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,\n+  options: {\n+    sessionId: string;\n+    status: AgentSessionStatus;\n+    turns: AgentTurn[];\n+    summary: string | null;\n+  },\n+): Promise<void> {\n+  const { error } = await supabaseAdmin\n+    .from(\"timeline_agent_sessions\")\n+    .update({\n+      status: options.status,\n+      turns: options.turns,\n+      summary: options.summary,\n+      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n+    })\n+    .eq(\"id\", options.sessionId);\n+\n+  if (error) {\n+    throw new Error(`Failed to persist session state: ${error.message}`);\n+  }\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

T14-T15 are validation.\n\n**Critical judgment call \u2014 error handling scope (flag-tool-error-handling-regression):** The plan's Step 7 proposed a blanket catch in executeTool. DO NOT DO THIS. The current error flow is: DB throw \u2192 outer loop catch \u2192 error turn pushed \u2192 session status set to 'error' \u2192 HTTP 500. This is correct behavior. The ONLY error-handling change is in createGenerationTask (T8): change throw to return. Everything else must continue to throw on failure.\n\n**Verified safe \u2014 duplicate turn removal (flag-agent-chat-message-user-hidden):** I read AgentChat.tsx:38-82 carefully. The frontend (1) skips all standalone tool_result turns (line 41-43), (2) groups tool_call + paired tool_result into accordion widgets, and (3) already skips the duplicate assistant turn via dedup at lines 58-70. So currently, message_user text ONLY appears inside the tool accordion \u2014 the duplicate assistant turn is already invisible. Removing it from the backend changes nothing visible. This is safe.\n\n**Deno import paths:** All imports use relative paths with .ts extensions (Deno convention). New modules must follow this: `import { foo } from \"./types.ts\"`, `import { bar } from \"./llm/client.ts\"`, etc. The shared imports use `../_shared/` paths.\n\n**The timelineState mutation pattern:** In the current executeTimelineTool (index.ts:426-497), the function MUTATES the timelineState object in place (state.config = ..., state.configVersion = ...). This is intentional \u2014 the loop uses a single timelineState reference across iterations. Preserve this mutation pattern in the registry.\n\n**File layout decision (settled):** db.ts is a separate module (user chose this). config.ts holds all tunables (user chose this). These differ from the plan which discussed alternatives.\n\n**What NOT to change:** prompts.ts stays as-is. The tool schema definitions (tools.ts \u2192 tool-schemas.ts) stay as-is except the rename. The withEdgeRequest handler pattern in index.ts stays the same. The HTTP response format stays the same."

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/loop.ts\n+import type { EdgeRuntime } from \"../_shared/edgeRequest.ts\";\n+import { toErrorMessage } from \"../_shared/errorMessage.ts\";\n+import {\n+  LOOP_LIMIT,\n+  OPENROUTER_MODEL,\n+  SOFT_TIMEOUT_MS,\n+} from \"./config.ts\";\n+import { loadSessionStatus, loadTimelineState, persistSessionState } from \"./db.ts\";\n+import { invokeLlmWithRetry } from \"./llm/client.ts\";\n+import {\n+  buildInitialMessages,\n+  createToolTurn,\n+  createTurn,\n+  extractAssistantText,\n+  isRecord,\n+  parseToolArgsSafely,\n+} from \"./llm/messages.ts\";\n+import { buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt } from \"./prompts.ts\";\n+import { TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS } from \"./tools.ts\";\n+import { executeTool } from \"./tools/registry.ts\";\n+import { viewTimeline } from \"./tools/timeline.ts\";\n+import type {\n+  AgentSession,\n+  AgentSessionStatus,\n+  AgentTurn,\n+  SupabaseAdmin,\n+} from \"./types.ts\";\n+\n+type LoopLogger = Pick<EdgeRuntime[\"logger\"], \"error\" | \"info\">;\n+\n+export interface RunAgentLoopOptions {\n+  session: AgentSession;\n+  userMessage?: string;\n+  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin;\n+  logger: LoopLogger;\n+}\n+\n+export interface RunAgentLoopResult {\n+  status: AgentSessionStatus;\n+  turns: AgentTurn[];\n+  summary: string | null;\n+}\n+\n+export async function runAgentLoop(\n+  options: RunAgentLoopOptions,\n+): Promise<RunAgentLoopResult> {\n+  const { session, userMessage, supabaseAdmin, logger } = options;\n+\n+  // Guard against duplicate user messages (client may retry or double-fire)\n+  const lastTurn = session.turns[session.turns.length - 1];\n+  const isDuplicate = userMessage\n+    && lastTurn?.role === \"user\"\n+    && lastTurn.content === userMessage;\n+  const turns = (userMessage && !isDuplicate)\n+    ? [...session.turns, createTurn(\"user\", userMessage)]\n+    : [...session.turns];\n+  let status: AgentSessionStatus = \"processing\";\n+  let summary = session.summary;\n+  const turnsBeforeInvocation = session.turns.length;\n+\n+  try {\n+    await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {\n+      sessionId: session.id,\n+      status,\n+      turns,\n+      summary,\n+    });\n+\n+    const timelineState = await loadTimelineState(supabaseAdmin, session.timeline_id);\n+    const timelineSummary = viewTimeline(timelineState.config, timelineState.registry).result;\n+    const systemPrompt = buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt({\n+      projectId: timelineState.projectId,\n+      timelineSummary,\n+    });\n+    const messages = buildInitialMessages(systemPrompt, turns);\n+    const startedAt = Date.now();\n+\n+    loop: for (let iteration = 0; iteration < LOOP_LIMIT; iteration += 1) {\n+      if (Date.now() - startedAt >= SOFT_TIMEOUT_MS) {\n+        status = \"continue\";\n+        break;\n+      }\n+\n+      const currentStatus = await loadSessionStatus(supabaseAdmin, session.id);\n+      if (currentStatus === \"cancelled\") {\n+        status = \"cancelled\";\n+        break;\n+      }\n+\n+      let completion;\n+      try {\n+        completion = await invokeLlmWithRetry(\n+          {\n+            model: OPENROUTER_MODEL,\n+            messages: messages as unknown[],\n+            tools: TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS as unknown[],\n+            tool_choice: \"auto\",\n+            temperature: 0.2,\n+            max_tokens: 4096,\n+            top_p: 1,\n+          },\n+          logger,\n+        );\n+      } catch (llmError: unknown) {\n+        const text = `The timeline agent hit an AI service error: ${toErrorMessage(llmError)}. Please retry in a moment.`;\n+        turns.push(createTurn(\"assistant\", text));\n+        messages.push({ role: \"assistant\", content: text });\n+        status = \"waiting_user\";\n+        break;\n+      }\n+\n+      const responseMessage = completion.choices[0]?.message;\n+      if (!responseMessage) {\n+        turns.push(createTurn(\"assistant\", \"I couldn't produce a response.\"));\n+        status = \"waiting_user\";\n+        break;\n+      }\n+\n+      const assistantText = extractAssistantText(responseMessage);\n+      const rawToolCalls = Array.isArray((responseMessage as { tool_calls?: unknown[] }).tool_calls)\n+        ? ((responseMessage as { tool_calls: unknown[] }).tool_calls as unknown[])\n+        : [];\n+      const toolCalls = rawToolCalls.flatMap((toolCall) => {\n+        if (!isRecord(toolCall)) {\n+          return [];\n+        }\n+\n+        const fn = isRecord(toolCall.function) ? toolCall.function : undefined;\n+        if (!fn || typeof fn.name !== \"string\") {\n+          return [];\n+        }\n+\n+        return [{\n+          id: typeof toolCall.id === \"string\" ? toolCall.id : crypto.randomUUID(),\n+          type: \"function\" as const,\n+          function: {\n+            name: fn.name,\n+            arguments: typeof fn.arguments === \"string\" ? fn.arguments : \"{}\",\n+          },\n+        }];\n+      });\n+\n+      if (toolCalls.length === 0) {\n+        const text = assistantText || \"I need a bit more direction before changing the timeline.\";\n+        turns.push(createTurn(\"assistant\", text));\n+        messages.push({ role: \"assistant\", content: text });\n+        status = \"waiting_user\";\n+        break;\n+      }\n+\n+      messages.push({\n+        role: \"assistant\",\n+        content: assistantText || null,\n+        tool_calls: toolCalls,\n+      });\n+\n+      for (const toolCall of toolCalls) {\n+        if (Date.now() - startedAt >= SOFT_TIMEOUT_MS) {\n+          status = \"continue\";\n+          break loop;\n+        }\n+\n+        const currentStatusBeforeTool = await loadSessionStatus(supabaseAdmin, session.id);\n+        if (currentStatusBeforeTool === \"cancelled\") {\n+          status = \"cancelled\";\n+          break loop;\n+        }\n+\n+        const toolName = toolCall.function.name;\n+        const { args: toolArgs, error: toolArgsError } = parseToolArgsSafely(toolCall.function.arguments);\n+        turns.push(createToolTurn(\n+          \"tool_call\",\n+          toolName,\n+          `Called ${toolName}.`,\n+          toolArgs,\n+        ));\n+\n+        const toolResult = toolArgsError\n+          ? { result: toolArgsError }\n+          : await executeTool(\n+            toolName,\n+            toolArgs,\n+            {\n+              config: timelineState.config,\n+              registry: timelineState.registry,\n+              projectId: timelineState.projectId,\n+            },\n+            timelineState,\n+            session.timeline_id,\n+            supabaseAdmin,\n+          );\n+\n+        turns.push(createToolTurn(\"tool_result\", toolName, toolResult.result, toolArgs));\n+        messages.push({\n+          role: \"tool\",\n+          tool_call_id: toolCall.id,\n+          content: toolResult.result,\n+        });\n+\n+        if (\"stopLoop\" in toolResult && toolResult.stopLoop) {\n+          status = toolResult.nextStatus ?? \"waiting_user\";\n+          if (toolName === \"done\") {\n+            summary = toolResult.result;\n+          }\n+          break loop;\n+        }\n+      }\n+    }\n+\n+    if (status === \"processing\") {\n+      status = \"continue\";\n+    }\n+\n+    logger.info(\"Agent loop completed\", {\n+      session_id: session.id,\n+      status,\n+      turns_added: turns.length - turnsBeforeInvocation,\n+      tool_count: TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS.length,\n+    });\n+\n+    return {\n+      status,\n+      turns,\n+      summary,\n+    };\n+  } catch (error: unknown) {\n+    const errorDetail = toErrorMessage(error);\n+    console.error(`[agent] FATAL: ${errorDetail}`);\n+    logger.error(\"Agent loop failed\", {\n+      session_id: session.id,\n+      error: errorDetail,\n+    });\n+\n+    // Write the error into turns so it's visible from DB inspection\n+    turns.push(createTurn(\"assistant\", `[INTERNAL ERROR] ${errorDetail}`));\n+\n+    try {\n+      await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {\n+        sessionId: session.id,\n+        status: \"error\",\n+        turns,\n+        summary,\n+      });\n+    } catch (persistError: unknown) {\n+      logger.error(\"Failed to persist error state\", {\n+        session_id: session.id,\n+        error: toErrorMessage(persistError),\n+      });\n+    }\n+\n+    throw error;\n+  }\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Delete File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts\n+import { serve } from \"https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts\";\n+import { NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS, withEdgeRequest } from \"../_shared/edgeHandler.ts\";\n+import { toErrorMessage } from \"../_shared/errorMessage.ts\";\n+import { jsonResponse } from \"../_shared/http.ts\";\n+import { persistSessionState } from \"./db.ts\";\n+import { isRecord, normalizeSessionRow } from \"./llm/messages.ts\";\n+import { runAgentLoop } from \"./loop.ts\";\n+import { TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS } from \"./tools.ts\";\n+import type { AgentInvocationBody } from \"./types.ts\";\n+\n+serve((req) => withEdgeRequest<AgentInvocationBody>(\n+  req,\n+  {\n+    functionName: \"ai-timeline-agent\",\n+    logPrefix: \"[AI-TIMELINE-AGENT]\",\n+    method: \"POST\",\n+    parseBody: \"strict\",\n+    auth: { required: true, options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true } },\n+    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,\n+  },\n+  async ({ auth, body, logger, supabaseAdmin }) => {\n+    if (!auth?.userId) return jsonResponse({ error: \"Authentication failed\" }, 401);\n+    const sessionId = typeof body.session_id === \"string\" ? body.session_id.trim() : \"\";\n+    const userMessage = body.user_message === undefined || body.user_message === null\n+      ? undefined\n+      : typeof body.user_message === \"string\" ? body.user_message.trim() : null;\n+    if (!sessionId) return jsonResponse({ error: \"session_id is required\" }, 400);\n+    if (userMessage === null) return jsonResponse({ error: \"user_message must be a string when provided\" }, 400);\n+\n+    const { data: rawSession, error: sessionError } = await supabaseAdmin.from(\"timeline_agent_sessions\").select(\"*\").eq(\"id\", sessionId).maybeSingle();\n+    if (sessionError) {\n+      logger.error(\"Failed to load session\", { session_id: sessionId, error: sessionError.message });\n+      return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to load session\" }, 500);\n+    }\n+    if (!rawSession || !isRecord(rawSession)) return jsonResponse({ error: \"Session not found\" }, 404);\n+\n+    const session = normalizeSessionRow(rawSession);\n+    if (session.user_id !== auth.userId) return jsonResponse({ error: \"Forbidden\" }, 403);\n+    if (session.status === \"cancelled\") return jsonResponse({ error: \"Session cancelled\", status: \"cancelled\" }, 409);\n+\n+    const lastTurn = session.turns[session.turns.length - 1];\n+    const nextUserMessage = userMessage && lastTurn?.role === \"user\" && lastTurn.content === userMessage ? undefined : userMessage;\n+\n+    try {\n+      const result = await runAgentLoop({ session, userMessage: nextUserMessage, supabaseAdmin, logger });\n+      await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {\n+        sessionId: session.id,\n+        status: result.status,\n+        turns: result.turns,\n+        summary: result.summary,\n+      });\n+      return jsonResponse({\n+        session_id: session.id,\n+        status: result.status,\n+        turns_added: result.turns.length - session.turns.length,\n+        tool_count: TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS.length,\n+        model: session.model,\n+      });\n+    } catch (error: unknown) {\n+      return jsonResponse({\n+        error: \"Failed to process timeline agent request\",\n+        details: toErrorMessage(error),\n+      }, 500);\n+    }\n+  },\n+));\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Refactor ai-timeline-agent edge function from 840-line monolith to clean modular structure: split into loop/tools/llm/session modules, unified tool interface, remove dead Llama fallback code, fix message_user duplication, consistent error handling

User notes and answers:
- This is a LIGHT refactor — pure restructuring, no new features. The function works correctly now. Goals:

1. Split index.ts (~840 lines) into focused modules
2. Unified tool interface: every tool implements (args, context) => ToolResult
3. Remove dead Llama fallback parser (we use OpenRouter/minimax now, not Groq/Llama)
4. Fix message_user creating duplicate turns (tool_result + assistant with same content)
5. Consistent error handling (session tools should return errors, not throw)
6. Remove hand-typed Supabase client interfaces — use a shared context type
7. Clean tool dispatch — single registry, no if/else special-casing

Target structure:
  ai-timeline-agent/
    index.ts              — serve() handler only (~50 lines)
    loop.ts               — LLM loop logic  
    tools/registry.ts     — tool registration + dispatch
    tools/timeline.ts     — timeline manipulation tools (moved from timeline-tools.ts)
    tools/session.ts      — message_user, done (moved from session-tools.ts)
    tools/generation.ts   — create_generation_task (extracted from session-tools.ts)
    llm/client.ts         — OpenRouter client
    llm/messages.ts       — turn <-> LLM message conversion
    prompts.ts            — stays as-is
    tools.ts              — tool schema definitions (stays as-is, rename to tool-schemas.ts)

Key constraint: behavior must be identical after refactor. Same API contract, same tool behavior, same turn format in DB. This is a pure internal restructuring.

Existing files:
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts (840+ lines, the monolith)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools.ts (tool schema definitions)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/timeline-tools.ts (timeline manipulation)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/session-tools.ts (session tools)
- supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts (system prompt)
- Answers: Q1: The frontend AgentChat already deduplicates — buildRenderedTurns skips assistant turns that match a preceding message_user tool_result. So removing the duplicate assistant turn from the backend is safe. Q2: Separate db.ts — cleaner. Q3: config.ts with all tunables — worth the extra file for discoverability.
